# MPL_147_048-137: doubts, readings, and abbreviations

## Scope and use of this register

This register enumerates all 623 surviving doubt-macro occurrences in the 90 page fragments: 620 occurrences of `\unsure` and 3 of `\illegible`. Nested macros receive separate entries. Each occurrence has one catalog identifier in an HTML comment on its table row.

The reading column reproduces the complete macro, with whitespace folded to one line for the table; words, symbols, braces, and encoded alternatives are unchanged. For `\unsure{reading|alternative}`, the first top-level segment is the retained reading. Alternatives are separated only at an unescaped pipe outside nested brace groups; nested formulas, escaped braces, and nested doubt macros remain intact.

All positions are estimates. A metadata line or position locator is reproduced when a specific reading could be matched, together with a local passage anchor. The anchors are excerpts from the transcription, not fresh measurements of the manuscript. TeX source line numbers are not manuscript line numbers and are not used as locators in these tables.

Confidence is inherited only from a sufficiently specific metadata match. The 426 occurrences without such a match receive the explicitly labelled conservative default `low`; this is not a new visual assessment. When matched records disagree, the lowest recorded label is shown. Metadata records that cannot be assigned securely are preserved separately below; some may describe a passage that was revised during the final visual review.

## Legibility and technical limits

The page records place the greatest reading uncertainty in dense cancellations, overwritten letters and numerals, compressed marginal additions, small indices and superscripts, accents and prime marks, and diagram labels or arrowheads. These are the parts to trust least without returning to the scans. A plausible mathematical expression is not evidence for changing the ink-based reading. The register identifies marked doubts; it does not certify that every possible misreading has been detected.

No full LaTeX compilation was completed: the checking environment lacks required TeX packages. Structural checks and the occurrence catalog do not establish that every diagram typesets correctly or that the fragment will compile in a complete TeX environment.
